# The recruiting talks line run (Cain 2026-11-12, 20,000 lines for the recruiting personal talks). Written through the Message Batches API and checked by
# tools/linebank (check.py and one model pass); merged here by `run.py --job recruit20k merge`. Format in src/world/folk/voice.rs.
# portal.* are asked by the game today; the rest wait for the talk design (docs/design/recruit-talk-lines.md).

@key portal.ask.development
Will your staff coach me on the little things, or just the plays?
How many guys from my spot have you put in the league?
I want to know what you'd work on with me first.
I stalled out last time. What makes this place different?
Show me one player you made better, not one you signed that way.
Is the coach I'd work with every day any good at teaching?
What do your guys look like when they leave here?
Do you break down film with players one on one?
Can I count on real feedback, even when it's bad?
What does a normal week of coaching look like for someone like me?
Tell me how you get a guy ready for the next level.
I need to grow, not just play. Is that something you do here?
Who did you turn into a pro that nobody saw coming?
Do the scouts come through here, and do they leave knowing my name?
What's the plan to get my tape in front of the right people?
Are you going to push me past where I am now?
I'm not looking for a spot. I'm looking for somebody to make me better.
How much time do your coaches spend on the guys who aren't stars yet?
Where do your players end up after they're done here?
Do I get a real plan for my game, or a general one for the whole room?
Honest question. Will I be better in a year than I am today?
What do the guys you've sent on to the league say about their coaches here?
Can you teach me what I never learned before?
I want a coach who's been where I'm trying to go. Who's that for me?
What's my film going to look like at the end of next season?
Do you grade every rep, or just the games?
I've got a lot of ability. I need somebody to shape it. Is that you?
What's the biggest thing you'd fix in the way I play?
Who watches my tape with me and tells me what's wrong?
Are the scouts here every week or just once in a while?
I want to be a pro. Walk me through how you'd get me there.
Has your staff ever taken a guy like me and made him a draft pick?
Tell me about somebody who came in rough and left ready.
I need technique work, not just reps. Do I get that?
What can you show me that proves you build players up?
How do you help a guy who's behind get caught up?
I'm coming from a place that didn't coach me much. Will you?
Tell me what your staff sees in me that I can still improve.
Can I see what your guys looked like when they first got here?
I want my game cleaned up. Can your position coach do it?
Does your staff stay on guys, or do they let them coast?
I don't want a cheerleader. I want somebody who'll tell me the truth about my game.
How do you prepare a guy for the draft process?
If I do my part, where do you see my game in two years?
What's your pitch to a guy who just wants to get better?
I need to leave here a different player. Can you promise that much?
Honestly, how good is your coaching at my position? ~ qb
Have you ever turned a back into a pro? ~ rb
What do your receivers learn here that they don't learn anywhere else? ~ wr
Do tight ends actually get coached up here, or just used? ~ te
Who's coaching the line, and what has he done for guys up front? ~ ol
How does your staff teach pass rushers to finish? ~ rusher
Do your linebackers leave here ready for the next level? ~ lb
Tell me what your coaches have done for defensive backs. ~ db
Do you develop kickers, or do you just hope they show up good? ~ kicker

How does your defense turn players into pros? ~ defense
Do guys in the skill spots actually get better here? ~ skill
What does your staff do for the big guys up front? ~ lineman
How do your coaches teach guys in coverage? ~ cover
Prove to me you can make me better. I've heard it before. ~ ego
I'm better than what I showed, and I need a coach who can pull it out of me. ~ ego
I want to be great. Can your coaches get me there? ~ competitor
I don't care about the easy path. Who's going to push me? ~ competitor
Whatever you tell me to fix, I'll work on it. What would that be? ~ humble
I know I've got holes in my game. Can you help me fill them? ~ humble
Look, I don't need hand-holding. I need real coaching. ~ hot
Don't give me a sales pitch. Tell me how I get better. ~ hot
Take me through how you'd build my game. ~ calm
I've got time. Tell me exactly how you'd coach me. ~ calm
I'd stay a long time if you actually make me better. ~ loyal
Does your staff stick with players, or move on when someone better shows up? ~ loyal
Is the next level going to pay off for a guy who comes here? ~ greedy
I'm trying to get paid someday. What do your coaches do for that? ~ greedy
I take the work seriously. Does your staff match that? ~ pro
I'll put in the hours. Will your coaches put in theirs? ~ pro
I haven't had the best coaching, so I'm hoping that changes. ~ shaky
What's the process for a player like me to get better? ~ steady
Who's going to teach me what I don't know yet? ~ young
I'm still learning. Will your staff be patient and keep teaching? ~ young
I've seen a lot of coaches. What makes yours worth my last years? ~ vet
I've been around. Do your guys still teach, or are they all talk? ~ vet
Where I came from, I got stuck. How do I not stay stuck here? ~ transfer
I'm a new guy to your system. How do you get me up to speed? ~ transfer
Can your staff make my film look better for the pros? ~ modern
Do you put guys' clips up so scouts can find them? ~ modern
I'm weighing NIL, but I care about getting better first. Can you do that? ~ nil
The money's good, but will I develop? ~ nil

What do scouts say about your program when they walk through?

What's your plan for me if I'm already good? Do I get pushed?
Who on your staff has actually played at the next level?
I don't want to just play. I want to leave better. How do you make that happen?
What does a normal week of film look like around here?

Has your staff turned a guy like me into a pro before?

@key portal.ask.family
Before I pick a school, who on your staff is going to be straight with me?
How do you handle it when a player and a coach don't see eye to eye?
I want to hear how your assistants talk about their players when we're not around.
Can I talk to a few guys on your roster without you in the room?
Is this a staff that sticks together or one that points fingers when things go bad?

I'm not asking about your offense yet. I'm asking about the people.
Tell me about a time you stood up for a player.
How does your staff handle a guy who made a mistake?
